{"forms":[{"form":"babies","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A very young human, particularly from birth to a couple of years old or until walking is fully mastered."]},{"examples":[{"text":"When is your baby due?","type":"example"},{"text":"Her baby had always been active, even before he was born, when he would kick her bladder.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A very young human, particularly from birth to a couple of years old or until walking is fully mastered.","A very young human, even if not yet born."]},{"glosses":["Any very young animal, especially a vertebrate; many species have specific names for their babies, such as kittens for the babies of cats, puppies for the babies of dogs, and chicks for the babies of birds. See :Category:Baby animals for more."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Stand up for yourself – don't be such a baby!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person who is immature, infantile, or feeble."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I only qualified as an architect this summer, so I'm still a baby.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person who is new to or inexperienced in something."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Adam is the baby of the family.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The lastborn of a family; the youngest sibling, irrespective of age."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Too busy thinking about my baby, and I ain't got time for nothing else.","type":"example"},{"text":"Baby, don't cry.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person's romantic partner; a term of endearment used to refer to or address e.g. one's girlfriend, boyfriend, or spouse."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Hey baby, what are you doing later?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A form of address to a person considered to be attractive."],"tags":["informal"]},{"glosses":["A thing that is comparatively small of its kind."]},{"examples":[{"text":"This test program I've designed is my new baby.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A concept or creation endeared by its creator."]},{"examples":[{"text":"You need to talk to John about that – it's his baby.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A pet project or responsibility."]},{"examples":[{"text":"See my new car here? I can't wait to take this baby for a drive.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["An affectionate term for anything."]},{"glosses":["A small image of an infant; a doll."],"tags":["archaic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"baby queer; baby trans","type":"example"}],"glosses":["One who is new to an identity or community."],"tags":["attributive","often"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"bā'bē"},{"ipa":"/ˈbeɪ.bi/","tags":["General-American","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈbɛj.bɪj/","tags":["British","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/ˈbæɪ.bi/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]},{"ipa":"/ˈbeːbi/","tags":["Wales"]},{"ipa":"/ˈbe.be/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/-bɪ/","tags":["Scotland"]},{"ipa":"/-bi/","tags":["Scotland"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"young human being","word":"babe"},{"word":"babby"},{"word":"babbie"},{"word":"infant"},{"sense":"young animal","word":"youngling"},{"sense":"immature or infantile person","word":"big baby"},{"sense":"term of endearment","word":"love"},{"tags":["UK"],"word":"bab"},{"tags":["UK"],"word":"babber"},{"word":"babe in arms"},{"word":"baby"},{"tags":["Scotland"],"word":"bairn"},{"tags":["obsolete","slang"],"word":"bantling"},{"tags":["Australia","slang"],"word":"bub"},{"word":"bundle of joy"},{"word":"chit"},{"tags":["derogatory","slang"],"word":"crotch dropping"},{"tags":["derogatory","slang"],"word":"crotchfruit"},{"tags":["derogatory","slang"],"word":"fleshloaf"},{"word":"nursling"},{"word":"papoose"},{"tags":["humorous","pejorative"],"word":"poop factory"},{"tags":["humorous","pejorative"],"word":"poop machine"},{"word":"suckling"},{"tags":["humorous","pejorative"],"word":"shit factory"}],"upstream_line":12446,"upstream_offset":79090573,"upstream_record_sha256":"71f4b82307a0cbe11c9d64e8cc8404580e41c86d6430b3092a965c16ccb336f9","word":"baby"}
